Daybook Entries · A COMMUNION OF WIVES, 1587

4 Apr.

4 Apr. is part of Daybook Entries. A COMMUNION OF WIVES, 1587 - topics: travel,court_patronage,household_family,health_illness,money_legal,angelic_spiritual. Records travel, court patronage, household family, health illness, money legal: Actio Tertia increpit. 1 After our prayers to God, there was a long pause. I read aloud to God the Lord Rosenberg's letters (which I had received at last): so that my Lord's obedience, humility and aspirations might be witnessed by God and his angels. Records travel, court patronage, household family, angelic spiritual, alchemy experiment: Thursday, hora 174 a meridie. After E.T. his calling into the Stone, appeared a tall man with a sceptre (very great) of gold, glittering, his body all red: and out of his head did shoot out beams oflight, like the sunbeams. Records travel: At afternoon came one of Dr Curtz his servants from his master to tell me that his master would come to me tomorrow in the morning about 7,8 or 9 of the clock, as I would, &c. 6 Oct.

6 Oct. is part of Daybook Entries. WHO ARE YOU, THAT YOU REQUIRE SCIENCE?, 1584 - topics: travel,court_patronage,household_family,letters_books. Records travel, court patronage, household family, letters books: Friday, mane. Before 7 of the clock 1 thought good rather myself to go to Dr Curtz, than to suffer him to come to me so far, and that for divers causes. So I went to him, and came before he was ready. Source: John Dee Edward Fenton Diaries of John Dee Day Books.pdf, pages 176-177.
